# M1 ten raw examples

Written after the single verdict run (`verdict_m1.json` sha256 `a329ce9c…`) by `scratchpad/m1_run/raw_examples2.py`.
Poles: the 640 grid poles, the only poles whose top-50 contexts are saved (`samples/contexts.jsonl`).
cm_share = slots in the 5 code+math directories / filled slots, from `m1/counts.npz`.
Largest shift under M (cm_share(M) - cm_share(start_a)): grid indices [85, 529, 619, 511, 630]. Random, numpy `default_rng(0)` over the other 635: [172, 196, 324, 403, 539].
Each slot: rank, directory (code+math directories in bold), activation a, the 60 characters before the token, the token, 30 characters after.
Directory counts at the 640 grid poles were checked equal to `counts.npz` for every harvest (laptop gate check).
